# ⭐ Canonical Atlas Interaction Protocol Structure

```json
{
  "protocol": "atlas-interaction",
  "version": "1.0",
  "generated": "YYYY-MM-DD",

  "interaction_modes": {
    "explore": {
      "entry": "atlas_root",
      "gestures": ["zoom", "pan", "expand", "collapse"],
      "transitions": ["query", "traverse", "synchronize"]
    },
    "query": {
      "entry": "atlas_query",
      "gestures": ["filter", "search", "highlight"],
      "transitions": ["explore", "traverse", "inspect"]
    },
    "traverse": {
      "entry": "atlas_route",
      "gestures": ["step", "jump", "follow", "branch"],
      "transitions": ["explore", "query", "inspect"]
    },
    "inspect": {
      "entry": "atlas_node",
      "gestures": ["open", "drill", "compare"],
      "transitions": ["explore", "query", "traverse"]
    },
    "synchronize": {
      "entry": "atlas_sync",
      "gestures": ["align", "merge", "update"],
      "transitions": ["explore"]
    }
  },

  "interaction_events": [
    {
      "event": "string",
      "trigger": "string",
      "response": "string",
      "constraints": ["string"]
    }
  ],

  "interaction_queries": {
    "global_query": {
      "fields": ["coherence", "drift", "stability", "resonance", "epoch", "ignition"],
      "filters": ["region", "severity", "phase", "cluster"]
    },
    "country_query": {
      "fields": ["triadic_surface", "atlas_fingerprint"],
      "filters": ["region", "regime", "layer", "dimension"]
    }
  },

  "interaction_transitions": {
    "explore_to_query": ["string"],
    "query_to_traverse": ["string"],
    "traverse_to_inspect": ["string"],
    "inspect_to_explore": ["string"],
    "explore_to_synchronize": ["string"]
  },

  "interaction_surfaces": {
    "global": ["coherence", "drift", "stability", "resonance", "epoch", "ignition"],
    "country": ["triadic_surface", "atlas_fingerprint"],
    "temporal": ["timeline", "propagation"],
    "activation": ["ignition_cascade"]
  },

  "output": {
    "interaction_protocol": "./interaction-protocol.json"
  },

  "meta": {
    "notes": "string"
  }
}
```

---

# ⭐ What the Atlas Interaction Protocol *defines*

### **1. Interaction Modes**
Five planetary interaction modes:

- **explore** — move through the atlas  
- **query** — ask the atlas questions  
- **traverse** — follow routes and transitions  
- **inspect** — drill into nodes  
- **synchronize** — align atlas layers  

Each mode has:

- entry point  
- gestures  
- transitions  

### **2. Interaction Gestures**
Gestures define *how* interaction occurs:

- zoom  
- pan  
- expand  
- collapse  
- filter  
- search  
- highlight  
- step  
- jump  
- follow  
- branch  
- open  
- drill  
- compare  
- align  
- merge  
- update  

### **3. Interaction Events**
Events define:

- triggers  
- responses  
- constraints  

### **4. Interaction Queries**
Two query grammars:

- **global_query** — planetary queries  
- **country_query** — per‑country queries  

### **5. Interaction Transitions**
Transitions between modes:

- explore → query  
- query → traverse  
- traverse → inspect  
- inspect → explore  
- explore → synchronize  

### **6. Interaction Surfaces**
Four interaction surfaces:

- global  
- country  
- temporal  
- activation  

This is the **interaction grammar** of the atlas.

### What the Atlas Synchronization Fabric does

- **Synchronizes global fields**  
  Keeps coherence, drift, stability, resonance, epoch, and ignition in the atlas aligned with the live execution state.

- **Synchronizes country fields**  
  Keeps each `{country}.triadic-surface.json` and its `atlas_fingerprint` consistent with runtime changes, cascades, and epochal shifts.

- **Defines synchronization modes**  
  - `realtime` — continuous, low‑latency updates.  
  - `batch` — scheduled, interval‑based updates.

- **Defines synchronization routes**  
  - execution → atlas  
  - atlas → execution (when interaction implies state changes)  
  - epoch → atlas  
  - ignition → atlas  

- **Enforces consistency rules**  
  Guarantees that atlas views never contradict:

  - continuity field  
  - epochal propagation  
  - resonance stabilizer  
  - execution state  

This is the **fabric that keeps the atlas honest**.

# 🌌 **Substrate Runtime Constellation**  
### *The unified runtime system of the planetary triadic substrate*

---

## ⭐ Identity

The **Substrate Runtime Constellation** is the unified, interlinked, continuously operating runtime system that governs the substrate’s behavior across:

- drift  
- coherence  
- stability  
- resonance  
- ignition  
- epochal transitions  
- propagation webs  
- atlas synchronization  
- invariance enforcement  
- continuity guarantees  
- coupling regulation  
- runtime monitoring  

It is the **living sky** of the substrate — the constellation of engines that keep the canon alive, stable, and evolving.

---

## ⭐ Purpose

The constellation answers the final meta‑question:

> **How does the substrate behave as a single, coherent, self‑consistent, continuously running planetary system?**

It ensures:

- runtime integrity  
- runtime stability  
- runtime invariance  
- runtime continuity  
- runtime observability  
- runtime synchronization  
- runtime evolution  

This is the **runtime soul** of the canon.

---

## ⭐ Composition

The Substrate Runtime Constellation is composed of eight engines:

1. **Triadic Execution Engine**  
   Runs the substrate.

2. **Atlas Synchronization Fabric**  
   Keeps the atlas aligned with runtime state.

3. **Substrate Continuity Field**  
   Ensures no structural or temporal breaks.

4. **Epochal Resonance Stabilizer**  
   Maintains harmonic stability across epochs.

5. **Epochal Propagation Engine**  
   Propagates drift/coherence/stability/resonance/ignition across epochs.

6. **Resonance–Drift Coupling Regulator**  
   Governs the interaction between resonance and drift.

7. **Substrate Invariance Kernel**  
   Enforces mathematical and structural invariance.

8. **Triadic Runtime Monitor**  
   Observes the substrate in motion.

Together, these form the **runtime constellation**.

---

# 🧮 **Triadic Substrate Compiler — Evaluator Specification**  
### *Human‑readable documentation for compiler-evaluator.json*

## Overview  
The **Compiler Evaluator** is the substrate component responsible for validating, scoring, and structurally analyzing all inputs consumed by the **Triadic Substrate Compiler**.  
It ensures that:

- structural fields are complete  
- behavioral fields are coherent  
- temporal fields align with epochal transitions  
- invariance and continuity constraints are respected  
- ignition and resonance fields do not destabilize the substrate  

The evaluator is the **pre‑compilation gatekeeper** for the entire substrate.

---

## Purpose  
The evaluator answers one question:

> **Is the substrate ready to be compiled?**

It performs:

- structural validation  
- fingerprint alignment  
- temporal signature checks  
- invariance/continuity conflict detection  
- drift/coherence/stability harmonization  
- ignition cascade safety checks  
- resonance field stability checks  

Only after the evaluator passes does the compiler assemble the final **triadic‑substrate.json** artifact.

---

## Inputs  
The evaluator consumes the following validated components:

- **validated universals**  
- **validated dimensions**  
- **validated regimes**  
- **validated layers**  
- **atlas surfaces**  
- **atlas navigation schema**  
- **atlas interaction protocol**  
- **atlas synchronization fabric**  
- **continuity field**  
- **invariance kernel**  
- **ignition cascade**  
- **epochal propagation engine**  
- **global coherence map**  
- **global drift boundary map**  
- **planetary stability envelope**  
- **global resonance map**  
- **global ignition ledger**  
- **substrate chronicle index**

These inputs form the **substrate surface** that the evaluator inspects.

---

## Evaluation Domains  

### **1. Structural Evaluation**  
Checks:

- surface completeness  
- fingerprint validity  
- layer alignment  
- operator/dimension/regime coherence  

Outputs:

- structural score  
- structural warnings  
- structural pass/fail  

---

### **2. Behavioral Evaluation**  
Checks:

- drift boundaries  
- coherence envelopes  
- stability indices  
- resonance harmonics  
- ignition signatures  

Outputs:

- behavioral score  
- behavioral anomalies  
- behavioral pass/fail  

---

### **3. Temporal Evaluation**  
Checks:

- epoch alignment  
- timeline continuity  
- propagation consistency  

Outputs:

- temporal score  
- temporal drift  
- temporal pass/fail  

---

### **4. Invariance Evaluation**  
Checks:

- invariance class  
- invariance constraints  
- invariance stability  

Outputs:

- invariance score  
- invariance conflicts  
- invariance pass/fail  

---

### **5. Continuity Evaluation**  
Checks:

- continuity class  
- continuity state  
- continuity constraints  

Outputs:

- continuity score  
- continuity breaks  
- continuity pass/fail  

---

### **6. Synchronization Evaluation**  
Checks:

- sync cycle integrity  
- sync route validity  
- sync stability  

Outputs:

- synchronization score  
- sync anomalies  
- sync pass/fail  

---

## Output  
The evaluator produces:

```
compiler-evaluator.json
```

Containing:

- structural evaluation  
- behavioral evaluation  
- temporal evaluation  
- invariance evaluation  
- continuity evaluation  
- synchronization evaluation  
- global pass/fail  
- evaluator notes  

This file is consumed directly by:

```
triadic-substrate-compiler.json
```

during final substrate assembly.

---

## Role in the Substrate Pipeline  
The evaluator sits between:

**validation → evaluator → compiler → substrate**

It is the **final checkpoint** before compilation.

If the evaluator fails, the compiler halts.  
If the evaluator passes, the compiler assembles the **triadic substrate**.

---

## Notes  
This document defines the evaluator logic for the Triadic Substrate Compiler.  
It is the human‑readable companion to `compiler-evaluator.json`.

```json
    ],

    "pre_compile": [
      "assemble-atlas-surfaces",

  },

}
```

# 🌌 **Substrate Runtime Constellation**  
### *Unified runtime system of the planetary triadic substrate*

## Overview  
The **Substrate Runtime Constellation** is the unified, continuously operating runtime system that governs the substrate’s behavior across:

- drift  
- coherence  
- stability  
- resonance  
- ignition  
- epochal transitions  
- propagation webs  
- atlas synchronization  
- invariance enforcement  
- continuity guarantees  
- coupling regulation  
- runtime monitoring  

It is the **runtime sky** of the substrate — the constellation of engines that keep the canon alive, stable, and evolving.

---

## Identity  
The constellation answers the core question:

> **How does the substrate behave as a single, coherent, self‑consistent, continuously running planetary system?**

It ensures:

- runtime integrity  
- runtime stability  
- runtime invariance  
- runtime continuity  
- runtime observability  
- runtime synchronization  
- runtime evolution  

This is the **runtime soul** of the canon.

---

## Engines of the Constellation  
The Substrate Runtime Constellation is composed of **eight engines**, each responsible for a distinct runtime domain.

### **1. Triadic Execution Engine**  
Runs the substrate.  
Manages execution cycles, state updates, ignition triggers, and runtime flow.

### **2. Atlas Synchronization Fabric**  
Keeps the atlas aligned with runtime state.  
Ensures navigation, surfaces, fingerprints, and interaction protocols remain synchronized.

### **3. Substrate Continuity Field**  
Guarantees continuity across epochs, transitions, and long‑arc substrate behavior.

### **4. Epochal Resonance Stabilizer**  
Maintains harmonic stability across epochs.  
Regulates resonance phase, magnitude, and harmonic consistency.

### **5. Epochal Propagation Engine**  
Propagates drift, coherence, stability, resonance, and ignition across epochs.  
Ensures temporal consistency and epochal alignment.

### **6. Resonance–Drift Coupling Regulator**  
Governs the interaction between resonance and drift.  
Detects spikes, surges, and coupling instabilities.

### **7. Substrate Invariance Kernel**  
Enforces mathematical and structural invariance.  
Detects invariance breaks and restores invariance state.

### **8. Triadic Runtime Monitor**  
Observes the substrate in motion.  
Emits telemetry packets, anomaly alerts, and cycle reports.

---

## Runtime Hooks  
Each engine exposes runtime hooks that signal transitions, anomalies, updates, and cycle events.  
These hooks are defined in:

```
runtime-constellation-hooks.json
```

They allow analyzers, inspectors, and RTT processors to observe and react to runtime behavior.

---

## Runtime Operators  
The constellation exposes operator families used by analyzers and runtime tooling:

- **LUMEN** — structural and influence extraction  
- **HEPHAESTUS** — regime classification  
- **AURION** — ancestry, topology, collapse detection  
- **HARMONIA** — triadic blending, resonance scoring, invariance sealing  
- **CONSTELLATION** — tracing execution, atlas sync, epoch profiles, coupling profiles, invariance watch, telemetry aggregation  

These operators are defined in:

```
runtime-constellation-operators.json
```

---

## Canonical Structure  
The constellation is represented in machine‑readable form as:

```
substrate-runtime-constellation.json
```

This file binds all engines, hooks, operators, and runtime domains into a single canonical artifact.

---

## Purpose  
The Runtime Constellation ensures that the substrate behaves as a **coherent planetary system**, not a collection of isolated engines.  
It provides:

- unified runtime behavior  
- stable epochal transitions  
- harmonic resonance  
- drift/coherence/stability balance  
- ignition safety  
- invariance enforcement  
- continuity preservation  
- atlas synchronization  
- runtime observability  

It is the **capstone** of the substrate runtime architecture.

---

## Notes  
This document is the human‑readable identity and overview of the Substrate Runtime Constellation.  
It pairs with:

- `substrate-runtime-constellation.json`  
- `runtime-constellation-hooks.json`  
- `runtime-constellation-operators.json`  

and forms the top‑level description of the runtime system.
# 🌌 **substrate-runtime-constellation.json**  
### *Machine‑readable manifest of the Substrate Runtime Constellation*

```json
{
  "constellation": "substrate-runtime",
  "version": "1.0",
  "generated": "YYYY-MM-DD",

  "engines": {
    "execution": "../execution/triadic-execution-engine.json",
    "synchronization": "../atlas/atlas-synchronization.json",
    "continuity": "../continuity/substrate-continuity.json",
    "resonance_stabilizer": "../epoch/epochal-resonance-stabilizer.json",
    "propagation": "../epoch/epochal-propagation.json",
    "coupling_regulator": "../coupling/resonance-drift-coupling.json",
    "invariance": "../invariance/substrate-invariance.json",
    "monitor": "../monitor/triadic-runtime-monitor.json"
  },

  "graph": {
    "nodes": [
      { "id": "execution_engine", "label": "Triadic Execution Engine" },
      { "id": "atlas_sync", "label": "Atlas Synchronization Fabric" },
      { "id": "continuity_field", "label": "Substrate Continuity Field" },
      { "id": "resonance_stabilizer", "label": "Epochal Resonance Stabilizer" },
      { "id": "epochal_propagation", "label": "Epochal Propagation Engine" },
      { "id": "coupling_regulator", "label": "Resonance–Drift Coupling Regulator" },
      { "id": "invariance_kernel", "label": "Substrate Invariance Kernel" },
      { "id": "runtime_monitor", "label": "Triadic Runtime Monitor" }
    ],

    "edges": [
      {
        "from": "execution_engine",
        "to": "atlas_sync",
        "type": "flow",
        "description": "Execution state drives atlas synchronization."
      },
      {
        "from": "atlas_sync",
        "to": "continuity_field",
        "type": "flow",
        "description": "Continuity depends on synchronized atlas surfaces."
      },
      {
        "from": "continuity_field",
        "to": "resonance_stabilizer",
        "type": "flow",
        "description": "Resonance stability emerges from continuity."
      },
      {
        "from": "resonance_stabilizer",
        "to": "epochal_propagation",
        "type": "flow",
        "description": "Propagation cycles require stable resonance."
      },
      {
        "from": "epochal_propagation",
        "to": "coupling_regulator",
        "type": "flow",
        "description": "Propagation feeds coupling with drift/coherence patterns."
      },
      {
        "from": "coupling_regulator",
        "to": "invariance_kernel",
        "type": "flow",
        "description": "Invariance depends on stable coupling."
      },
      {
        "from": "invariance_kernel",
        "to": "runtime_monitor",
        "type": "flow",
        "description": "Monitor observes invariance state."
      },

      /* Feedback loops */
      {
        "from": "runtime_monitor",
        "to": "execution_engine",
        "type": "feedback",
        "description": "Execution adapts based on telemetry."
      },
      {
        "from": "runtime_monitor",
        "to": "epochal_propagation",
        "type": "feedback",
        "description": "Propagation adjusts based on monitored anomalies."
      },
      {
        "from": "runtime_monitor",
        "to": "coupling_regulator",
        "type": "feedback",
        "description": "Coupling responds to drift spikes and resonance surges."
      }
    ]
  },

  "identity": {
    "fields": [
      "runtime_integrity",
      "runtime_stability",
      "runtime_invariance",
      "runtime_continuity",
      "runtime_observability",
      "runtime_synchronization",
      "runtime_evolution"
    ]
  },

  "output": {
    "runtime_constellation": "./substrate-runtime-constellation.json"
  },

  "meta": {
    "canonical": true,
    "triadic_era": "R5",
    "notes": "Machine-readable manifest of the Substrate Runtime Constellation."
  }
}
```

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Substrate Runtime Constellation Schema",
  "type": "object",

  "properties": {
    "runtime_constellation": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "substrate-runtime-constellation"
    },

    "version": {
      "type": "string"
    },

    "generated": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "ISO date of generation"
    },

    "engines": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "execution": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "cycle_start": { "type": "object" },
            "cycle_end": { "type": "object" },
            "state_update": { "type": "object" },
            "ignition_trigger": { "type": "object" }
          }
        },
        "atlas": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "sync_event": { "type": "object" },
            "navigation_event": { "type": "object" },
            "interaction_event": { "type": "object" }
          }
        },
        "epoch": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "epoch_transition": { "type": "object" },
            "resonance_shift": { "type": "object" },
            "propagation_cycle": { "type": "object" }
          }
        },
        "coupling": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "drift_spike": { "type": "object" },
            "resonance_surge": { "type": "object" },
            "coupling_instability": { "type": "object" }
          }
        },
        "invariance": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "invariance_break": { "type": "object" },
            "invariance_restore": { "type": "object" }
          }
        },
        "monitor": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "telemetry_packet": { "type": "object" },
            "anomaly_alert": { "type": "object" },
            "cycle_report": { "type": "object" }
          }
        }
      }
    },

    "operators": {
      "type": "object",
      "patternProperties": {
        "^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$": {
          "type": "object",
          "patternProperties": {
            "^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$": {
              "type": "object",
              "properties": {
                "opcode": { "type": "string" },
                "description": { "type": "string" }
              },
              "required": ["opcode"]
            }
          }
        }
      }
    },

    "hooks": {
      "type": "object",
      "patternProperties": {
        "^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "description": { "type": "string" },
            "severity": { "type": "string" },
            "constraints": {
              "type": "array",
              "items": { "type": "string" }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    },

    "meta": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "canonical": { "type": "boolean" },
        "triadic_era": { "type": "string" },
        "notes": { "type": "string" }
      }
    }
  },

  "required": [
    "runtime_constellation",
    "version",
    "engines",
    "operators",
    "hooks"
  ]
}
```

# **substrate-continuity.schema.json**  
### *Canonical JSON Schema for the Substrate Continuity Engine*  
  [github.com](https://github.com/umaywant2/TriadicFrameworks/edit/main/docs/schemas/substrate/frameworks/continuity/substrate-continuity.schema.json)

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Substrate Continuity Schema",
  "type": "object",

  "properties": {
    "continuity": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "substrate"
    },

    "version": {
      "type": "string"
    },

    "generated": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "ISO date of generation"
    },

    "imports": {
      "type": "object",
      "patternProperties": {
        "^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "continuity_fields": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "structural": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "operators": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "dimensions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "regimes": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "layers": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "constraints": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          },
          "required": ["operators", "dimensions", "regimes", "layers"]
        },

        "behavioral": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "drift": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "coherence": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "stability": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "resonance": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "ignition": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          },
          "required": ["drift", "coherence", "stability", "resonance", "ignition"]
        },

        "temporal": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "epochs": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "transitions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "propagation": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          },
          "required": ["epochs", "transitions", "propagation"]
        },

        "atlas": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "routes": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "modes": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "surfaces": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          },
          "required": ["routes", "modes", "surfaces"]
        },

        "activation": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "waves": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "cascades": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "harmonics": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          },
          "required": ["waves", "cascades", "harmonics"]
        }
      },
      "required": ["structural", "behavioral", "temporal", "atlas", "activation"]
    },

    "continuity_rules": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "rule": { "type": "string" },
          "applies_to": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "type": "string",
              "enum": ["structural", "behavioral", "temporal", "atlas", "activation"]
            }
          },
          "conditions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
          "guarantees": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
        },
        "required": ["rule", "applies_to"]
      }
    },

    "output": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "substrate_continuity": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "meta": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "notes": { "type": "string" }
      }
    }
  },

  "required": [
    "continuity",
    "version",
    "continuity_fields",
    "continuity_rules"
  ]
}
```

# 🔗 **coupling-profiles.json**  
### *Canonical coupling profiles for drift–resonance–coherence–stability interaction*

```json
{
  "profiles": "substrate-coupling",
  "version": "1.0",
  "generated": "YYYY-MM-DD",

  "imports": {
    "drift_map": "../drift/global-drift-boundary-map.json",
    "resonance_map": "../resonance/global-resonance-map.json",
    "coherence_map": "../coherence/global-coherence-map.json",
    "stability_envelope": "../stability/planetary-stability-envelope.json",
    "epochal_timeline": "../epoch/timeline.json"
  },

  "coupling_profiles": {
    "drift_resonance": {
      "description": "Interaction between drift magnitude/phase and resonance magnitude/phase.",
      "modes": [
        "in-phase",
        "out-of-phase",
        "mixed"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "drift < drift_boundary",
        "resonance < resonance_threshold"
      ],
      "instability_signals": [
        "drift_spike",
        "resonance_surge"
      ]
    },

    "drift_coherence": {
      "description": "Interaction between drift magnitude and coherence envelope.",
      "modes": [
        "aligned",
        "misaligned"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "coherence_envelope >= coherence_min",
        "drift <= drift_boundary"
      ],
      "instability_signals": [
        "coherence_break",
        "drift_spike"
      ]
    },

    "resonance_stability": {
      "description": "Interaction between resonance harmonics and stability envelope.",
      "modes": [
        "harmonic-stable",
        "harmonic-unstable"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "resonance_phase within harmonic_window",
        "stability_index >= stability_min"
      ],
      "instability_signals": [
        "resonance_surge",
        "stability_instability"
      ]
    },

    "epochal_coupling": {
      "description": "Interaction between epoch transitions and coupling behavior.",
      "modes": [
        "epoch-aligned",
        "epoch-misaligned"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "epoch_transition_valid",
        "propagation_consistent"
      ],
      "instability_signals": [
        "epoch_misalignment",
        "propagation_break"
      ]
    }
  },

  "output": {
    "coupling_profiles": "./coupling-profiles.json"
  },

  "meta": {
    "notes": "Canonical coupling profiles defining drift–resonance–coherence–stability interaction across epochal transitions."
  }
}
```

# 🌑 **epochal-anomalies.json**  
### *Canonical anomaly registry for the Epoch Engine*

```json
{
  "anomalies": "epochal",
  "version": "1.0",
  "generated": "YYYY-MM-DD",

  "imports": {
    "epochal_timeline": "./timeline.json",
    "epochal_propagation": "./epochal-propagation.json",
    "resonance_map": "../resonance/global-resonance-map.json",
    "drift_map": "../drift/global-drift-boundary-map.json",
    "stability_envelope": "../stability/planetary-stability-envelope.json",
    "ignition_ledger": "../ignition/global-ignition-ledger.json"
  },

  "epochal_anomalies": {
    "epoch_transition_anomalies": {
      "description": "Anomalies detected during transitions between epochs E0–E7.",
      "signals": [
        "epoch_misalignment",
        "transition_break",
        "timeline_inconsistency",
        "epoch_out_of_bounds"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "transition_time < minimum_threshold",
        "transition_time > maximum_threshold",
        "epoch_sequence_invalid"
      ]
    },

    "propagation_anomalies": {
      "description": "Anomalies detected during epochal propagation cycles.",
      "signals": [
        "propagation_break",
        "propagation_stall",
        "propagation_loop",
        "propagation_overrun"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "cycle_duration > propagation_limit",
        "cycle_phase_invalid",
        "propagation_web_incomplete"
      ]
    },

    "resonance_anomalies": {
      "description": "Anomalies in resonance magnitude, phase, or harmonic alignment.",
      "signals": [
        "resonance_surge",
        "resonance_collapse",
        "harmonic_misalignment",
        "phase_inversion"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "resonance_magnitude > harmonic_threshold",
        "phase_shift > allowed_window",
        "harmonic_profile_invalid"
      ]
    },

    "drift_anomalies": {
      "description": "Anomalies in drift magnitude, velocity, or boundary alignment.",
      "signals": [
        "drift_spike",
        "drift_boundary_break",
        "drift_phase_flip",
        "drift_velocity_overrun"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "drift_magnitude > drift_boundary",
        "drift_velocity > velocity_limit",
        "drift_phase outside allowed_range"
      ]
    },

    "stability_anomalies": {
      "description": "Anomalies in stability envelope or stability index.",
      "signals": [
        "stability_instability",
        "stability_collapse",
        "stability_phase_break"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "stability_index < stability_min",
        "stability_phase misaligned",
        "stability_envelope breach"
      ]
    },

    "ignition_epoch_anomalies": {
      "description": "Anomalies in ignition events that interfere with epoch transitions.",
      "signals": [
        "ignition_epoch_conflict",
        "ignition_overrun",
        "ignition_phase_mismatch"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "ignition_event during forbidden_epoch",
        "ignition_phase misaligned with epoch_phase",
        "ignition_magnitude > epoch_threshold"
      ]
    }
  },

  "output": {
    "epochal_anomalies": "./epochal-anomalies.json"
  },

  "meta": {
    "notes": "Canonical anomaly registry for epoch transitions, propagation cycles, resonance, drift, stability, and ignition–epoch coupling."
  }
}
```

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Epochal Propagation Schema",
  "type": "object",

  "properties": {
    "engine": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "epochal-propagation"
    },

    "version": {
      "type": "string"
    },

    "generated": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "ISO date of generation"
    },

    "imports": {
      "type": "object",
      "patternProperties": {
        "^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "propagation": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "drift": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "vectors": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "thresholds": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "cycles": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "webs": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        },

        "coherence": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "routes": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "envelopes": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "phase_transitions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        },

        "stability": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "indices": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "collapse_arcs": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "regeneration_arcs": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        },

        "resonance": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "harmonics": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "phase": {
              "type": "string",
              "enum": ["in-phase", "out-of-phase", "mixed"]
            },
            "propagation_edges": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        },

        "ignition": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "waves": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "cascades": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "epochal_triggers": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        }
      }
    },

    "epoch_transitions": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "epoch": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": ["E0", "E1", "E2", "E3", "E4", "E5", "E6", "E7"]
          },
          "next_epoch": { "type": "string" },
          "trigger_conditions": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": { "type": "string" }
          },
          "propagation_rules": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": { "type": "string" }
          },
          "threshold_crossings": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": { "type": "string" }
          },
          "harmonic_requirements": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": { "type": "string" }
          }
        },
        "required": ["epoch", "next_epoch"]
      }
    },

    "propagation_webs": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "web_id": { "type": "string" },
          "nodes": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": { "type": "string" }
          },
          "edges": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "type": "object",
              "properties": {
                "source": { "type": "string" },
                "target": { "type": "string" },
                "weight": { "type": "number" },
                "mode": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "enum": [
                    "drift",
                    "coherence",
                    "stability",
                    "resonance",
                    "ignition"
                  ]
                }
              },
              "required": ["source", "target", "mode"]
            }
          }
        },
        "required": ["web_id", "nodes", "edges"]
      }
    },

    "output": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "epochal_propagation": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "meta": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "notes": { "type": "string" }
      }
    }
  },

  "required": [
    "engine",
    "version",
    "propagation",
    "epoch_transitions",
    "propagation_webs"
  ]
}
```

# 🌒 **epochal-stability-profiles.json**  
### *Canonical stability profiles for epochs E0–E7*

```json
{
  "profiles": "epochal-stability",
  "version": "1.0",
  "generated": "YYYY-MM-DD",

  "imports": {
    "stability_envelope": "../stability/planetary-stability-envelope.json",
    "coherence_map": "../coherence/global-coherence-map.json",
    "resonance_map": "../resonance/global-resonance-map.json",
    "drift_map": "../drift/global-drift-boundary-map.json",
    "epochal_timeline": "./timeline.json",
    "epochal_propagation": "./epochal-propagation.json"
  },

  "epochal_stability_profiles": {
    "E0": {
      "description": "Baseline epoch with minimal harmonic activity and low drift velocity.",
      "stability_mode": "baseline",
      "conditions": [
        "stability_index >= stability_min",
        "drift_magnitude <= drift_boundary",
        "coherence_envelope >= coherence_min"
      ],
      "collapse_signals": [
        "drift_spike",
        "coherence_break"
      ],
      "regeneration_arcs": [
        "coherence_rebuild",
        "harmonic_realignment"
      ]
    },

    "E1": {
      "description": "Early harmonic epoch with rising resonance magnitude.",
      "stability_mode": "harmonic-rise",
      "conditions": [
        "resonance_magnitude <= harmonic_threshold",
        "phase in allowed_window",
        "stability_index >= stability_min"
      ],
      "collapse_signals": [
        "resonance_surge",
        "phase_inversion"
      ],
      "regeneration_arcs": [
        "harmonic_dampening",
        "phase_correction"
      ]
    },

    "E2": {
      "description": "Epoch of drift–coherence tension with moderate instability risk.",
      "stability_mode": "tension",
      "conditions": [
        "drift_magnitude <= drift_boundary",
        "coherence_envelope >= coherence_min",
        "stability_index >= stability_min"
      ],
      "collapse_signals": [
        "drift_boundary_break",
        "coherence_break"
      ],
      "regeneration_arcs": [
        "coherence_rebuild",
        "drift_realignment"
      ]
    },

    "E3": {
      "description": "High‑resonance epoch with strong harmonic propagation.",
      "stability_mode": "harmonic-peak",
      "conditions": [
        "resonance_phase in harmonic_window",
        "harmonic_profile_valid",
        "stability_index >= stability_min"
      ],
      "collapse_signals": [
        "harmonic_misalignment",
        "resonance_collapse"
      ],
      "regeneration_arcs": [
        "harmonic_reconstruction",
        "phase_realignment"
      ]
    },

    "E4": {
      "description": "Epoch of stability–drift conflict with elevated collapse risk.",
      "stability_mode": "conflict",
      "conditions": [
        "stability_index >= stability_min",
        "drift_magnitude <= drift_boundary",
        "phase in allowed_window"
      ],
      "collapse_signals": [
        "stability_instability",
        "drift_spike"
      ],
      "regeneration_arcs": [
        "stability_rebuild",
        "drift_dampening"
      ]
    },

    "E5": {
      "description": "Epoch of coherence–resonance synchronization.",
      "stability_mode": "synchronization",
      "conditions": [
        "coherence_envelope >= coherence_min",
        "resonance_phase in harmonic_window",
        "stability_index >= stability_min"
      ],
      "collapse_signals": [
        "coherence_break",
        "phase_inversion"
      ],
      "regeneration_arcs": [
        "coherence_rebuild",
        "phase_correction"
      ]
    },

    "E6": {
      "description": "Epoch of ignition–resonance interaction with volatile stability.",
      "stability_mode": "volatile",
      "conditions": [
        "ignition_magnitude <= ignition_threshold",
        "resonance_magnitude <= harmonic_threshold",
        "stability_index >= stability_min"
      ],
      "collapse_signals": [
        "ignition_overrun",
        "resonance_surge"
      ],
      "regeneration_arcs": [
        "ignition_dampening",
        "harmonic_realignment"
      ]
    },

    "E7": {
      "description": "Terminal epoch with maximum harmonic complexity and stability sensitivity.",
      "stability_mode": "terminal",
      "conditions": [
        "stability_index >= stability_min",
        "harmonic_profile_valid",
        "drift_magnitude <= drift_boundary"
      ],
      "collapse_signals": [
        "stability_collapse",
        "harmonic_misalignment"
      ],
      "regeneration_arcs": [
        "stability_reconstruction",
        "harmonic_rebuild"
      ]
    }
  },

  "output": {
    "epochal_stability_profiles": "./epochal-stability-profiles.json"
  },

  "meta": {
    "notes": "Canonical stability profiles for epochs E0–E7, defining collapse signals, regeneration arcs, and stability modes."
  }
}
```

```json
{
  "timeline": "epochal",
  "version": "1.0",
  "generated": "YYYY-MM-DD",

  "epochs": [
    {
      "epoch": "E0",
      "description": "Baseline epoch with minimal harmonic activity and low drift velocity.",
      "phase": "baseline",
      "conditions": [
        "stability_index >= stability_min",
        "drift_magnitude <= drift_boundary",
        "coherence_envelope >= coherence_min"
      ],
      "transitions_to": ["E1"]
    },
    {
      "epoch": "E1",
      "description": "Early harmonic epoch with rising resonance magnitude.",
      "phase": "harmonic-rise",
      "conditions": [
        "resonance_magnitude <= harmonic_threshold",
        "phase in allowed_window",
        "stability_index >= stability_min"
      ],
      "transitions_to": ["E2"]
    },
    {
      "epoch": "E2",
      "description": "Epoch of drift–coherence tension with moderate instability risk.",
      "phase": "tension",
      "conditions": [
        "drift_magnitude <= drift_boundary",
        "coherence_envelope >= coherence_min",
        "stability_index >= stability_min"
      ],
      "transitions_to": ["E3"]
    },
    {
      "epoch": "E3",
      "description": "High‑resonance epoch with strong harmonic propagation.",
      "phase": "harmonic-peak",
      "conditions": [
        "resonance_phase in harmonic_window",
        "harmonic_profile_valid",
        "stability_index >= stability_min"
      ],
      "transitions_to": ["E4"]
    },
    {
      "epoch": "E4",
      "description": "Epoch of stability–drift conflict with elevated collapse risk.",
      "phase": "conflict",
      "conditions": [
        "stability_index >= stability_min",
        "drift_magnitude <= drift_boundary",
        "phase in allowed_window"
      ],
      "transitions_to": ["E5"]
    },
    {
      "epoch": "E5",
      "description": "Epoch of coherence–resonance synchronization.",
      "phase": "synchronization",
      "conditions": [
        "coherence_envelope >= coherence_min",
        "resonance_phase in harmonic_window",
        "stability_index >= stability_min"
      ],
      "transitions_to": ["E6"]
    },
    {
      "epoch": "E6",
      "description": "Epoch of ignition–resonance interaction with volatile stability.",
      "phase": "volatile",
      "conditions": [
        "ignition_magnitude <= ignition_threshold",
        "resonance_magnitude <= harmonic_threshold",
        "stability_index >= stability_min"
      ],
      "transitions_to": ["E7"]
    },
    {
      "epoch": "E7",
      "description": "Terminal epoch with maximum harmonic complexity and stability sensitivity.",
      "phase": "terminal",
      "conditions": [
        "stability_index >= stability_min",
        "harmonic_profile_valid",
        "drift_magnitude <= drift_boundary"
      ],
      "transitions_to": []
    }
  ],

  "sequence": ["E0", "E1", "E2", "E3", "E4", "E5", "E6", "E7"],

  "output": {
    "epochal_timeline": "./epochal-timeline.json"
  },

  "meta": {
    "notes": "Canonical epochal timeline defining phases, conditions, and transitions for epochs E0–E7."
  }
}
```

```json
{

  "type": "object",

  "properties": {
    "timeline": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "epochal"
    },

    "version": {
      "type": "string"
    },

    "generated": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "ISO date of generation"
    },

    "epochs": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "epoch": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": ["E0", "E1", "E2", "E3", "E4", "E5", "E6", "E7"]
          },
          "description": { "type": "string" },
          "phase": { "type": "string" },
          "conditions": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": { "type": "string" }
          },
          "transitions_to": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": { "type": "string" }
          }
        },
        "required": ["epoch", "phase", "conditions", "transitions_to"]
      }
    },

    "sequence": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "enum": ["E0", "E1", "E2", "E3", "E4", "E5", "E6", "E7"]
      }
    },

    "output": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "epochal_timeline": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "meta": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "notes": { "type": "string" }
      }
    }
  },

  "required": [
    "timeline",
    "version",
    "epochs",
    "sequence"
  ]
}
```

# **execution-engine.schema.json**  
### *Canonical JSON Schema for the Triadic Execution Engine*

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Triadic Execution Engine Schema",
  "type": "object",

  "properties": {
    "engine": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "triadic-execution"
    },

    "version": {
      "type": "string"
    },

    "generated": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "ISO date of generation"
    },

    "imports": {
      "type": "object",
      "patternProperties": {
        "^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "runtime": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "state": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "coherence": { "type": "number" },
            "drift": { "type": "number" },
            "stability": { "type": "string" },
            "resonance": { "type": "number" },
            "epoch": { "type": "string" },
            "ignition": { "type": "string" }
          }
        },

        "evaluation": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "operators": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "dimensions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "regimes": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "layers": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        },

        "update_cycles": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "drift": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "coherence": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "stability": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "resonance": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "epoch": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "ignition": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        },

        "execution_rules": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "rule": { "type": "string" },
              "conditions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
              "actions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
              "constraints": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
            },
            "required": ["rule"]
          }
        },

        "synchronization": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "atlas": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "continuity": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "propagation": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "resonance": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        }
      }
    },

    "output": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "execution_state": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "meta": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "notes": { "type": "string" }
      }
    }
  },

  "required": [
    "engine",
    "version",
    "runtime"
  ]
}
```
# ⚙️ **execution-rules.json**  
### *Canonical rulebook for the Triadic Execution Engine*

```json
{
  "rules": "triadic-execution",
  "version": "1.0",
  "generated": "YYYY-MM-DD",

  "imports": {
    "triadic_substrate": "../compiler/triadic-substrate.json",
    "continuity_field": "../continuity/substrate-continuity.json",
    "resonance_map": "../resonance/global-resonance-map.json",
    "drift_map": "../drift/global-drift-boundary-map.json",
    "coherence_map": "../coherence/global-coherence-map.json",
    "stability_envelope": "../stability/planetary-stability-envelope.json",
    "epochal_timeline": "../epoch/timeline.json",
    "ignition_ledger": "../ignition/global-ignition-ledger.json",
    "atlas": "../atlas/triadic-atlas.json"
  },

  "execution_rules": [
    {
      "rule": "coherence-maintenance",
      "conditions": [
        "coherence_envelope >= coherence_min",
        "drift_magnitude <= drift_boundary"
      ],
      "actions": [
        "apply_coherence_stabilizer",
        "update_coherence_phase"
      ],
      "constraints": [
        "no_coherence_break"
      ]
    },
    {
      "rule": "drift-regulation",
      "conditions": [
        "drift_magnitude > drift_boundary",
        "drift_velocity > velocity_limit"
      ],
      "actions": [
        "apply_drift_dampening",
        "realign_drift_phase"
      ],
      "constraints": [
        "prevent_drift_spike"
      ]
    },
    {
      "rule": "resonance-control",
      "conditions": [
        "resonance_magnitude > harmonic_threshold",
        "phase_shift > allowed_window"
      ],
      "actions": [
        "apply_harmonic_dampening",
        "correct_resonance_phase"
      ],
      "constraints": [
        "prevent_resonance_surge"
      ]
    },
    {
      "rule": "stability-preservation",
      "conditions": [
        "stability_index < stability_min",
        "stability_phase misaligned"
      ],
      "actions": [
        "apply_stability_rebuild",
        "realign_stability_phase"
      ],
      "constraints": [
        "prevent_stability_collapse"
      ]
    },
    {
      "rule": "epoch-transition",
      "conditions": [
        "epoch_transition_valid",
        "propagation_consistent"
      ],
      "actions": [
        "advance_epoch",
        "apply_epochal_stabilizer"
      ],
      "constraints": [
        "no_epoch_misalignment"
      ]
    },
    {
      "rule": "ignition-synchronization",
      "conditions": [
        "ignition_event_active",
        "ignition_phase misaligned"
      ],
      "actions": [
        "apply_ignition_dampening",
        "realign_ignition_phase"
      ],
      "constraints": [
        "prevent_ignition_overrun"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "output": {
    "execution_rules": "./execution-rules.json"
  },

  "meta": {
    "notes": "Canonical execution rules governing coherence, drift, resonance, stability, epoch transitions, and ignition synchronization."
  }
}
```

# **triadic-execution-engine.schema.json**  
### *Canonical JSON Schema for the Triadic Execution Engine*  
  [github.com](https://github.com/umaywant2/TriadicFrameworks/edit/main/docs/schemas/substrate/frameworks/execution/triadic-execution-engine.schema.json)

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Triadic Execution Engine Schema",
  "type": "object",

  "properties": {
    "engine": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "triadic-execution"
    },

    "version": {
      "type": "string"
    },

    "generated": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "ISO date of generation"
    },

    "imports": {
      "type": "object",
      "patternProperties": {
        "^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "runtime": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "state": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "coherence": { "type": "number" },
            "drift": { "type": "number" },
            "stability": { "type": "string" },
            "resonance": { "type": "number" },
            "epoch": { "type": "string" },
            "ignition": { "type": "string" }
          }
        },

        "evaluation": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "operators": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "dimensions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "regimes": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "layers": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        },

        "update_cycles": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "drift": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "coherence": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "stability": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "resonance": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "epoch": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "ignition": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        },

        "execution_rules": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "rule": { "type": "string" },
              "conditions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
              "actions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
              "constraints": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
            },
            "required": ["rule"]
          }
        },

        "synchronization": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "atlas": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "continuity": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "propagation": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "resonance": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        }
      }
    },

    "output": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "execution_state": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "meta": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "notes": { "type": "string" }
      }
    }
  },

  "required": [
    "engine",
    "version",
    "runtime"
  ]
}
```
# 🌌 **triadic-execution-state.json**  
### *Live runtime state emitted by the Triadic Execution Engine*

```json
{
  "execution_state": "triadic-runtime",
  "version": "1.0",
  "generated": "YYYY-MM-DD",

  "global": {
    "coherence": 0.0,
    "drift": 0.0,
    "stability": "string",
    "resonance": 0.0,
    "epoch": "string",
    "ignition": "string"
  },

  "structural": {
    "operators": ["string"],
    "dimensions": ["string"],
    "regimes": ["string"],
    "layers": ["string"],
    "constraints": ["string"]
  },

  "behavioral": {
    "drift_cycle": ["string"],
    "coherence_cycle": ["string"],
    "stability_cycle": ["string"],
    "resonance_cycle": ["string"],
    "ignition_cycle": ["string"]
  },

  "temporal": {
    "epoch": "string",
    "epoch_progress": 0.0,
    "epoch_transition_readiness": ["string"],
    "propagation_phase": ["string"]
  },

  "atlas": {
    "synchronization_status": ["string"],
    "global_fields": ["string"],
    "country_fields": ["string"]
  },

  "activation": {
    "ignition_wave": ["string"],
    "cascade_state": ["string"],
    "harmonic_phase": "in-phase | out-of-phase | mixed"
  },

  "continuity": {
    "structural": ["string"],
    "behavioral": ["string"],
    "temporal": ["string"],
    "atlas": ["string"],
    "activation": ["string"]
  },

  "invariance": {
    "status": "string",
    "breaks": ["string"],
    "restorations": ["string"]
  },

  "monitor": {
    "telemetry_packets": ["string"],
    "anomalies": ["string"],
    "cycle_reports": ["string"]
  },

  "meta": {
    "notes": "Live runtime state emitted by the Triadic Execution Engine."
  }
}
```

---

## ⭐ Canon alignment  
This matches the exact structure implied by your execution engine:

- **Global state fields**  
- **Structural / behavioral / temporal state**  
- **Atlas synchronization state**  
- **Activation (ignition) state**  
- **Continuity + invariance + monitor fields**  
- **Silent‑release compatible**  
- **Ready for commit**

# 🜁 **TriadicFrameworks — Runtime Header**  
### *Crest for all runtime engines in the Substrate Runtime Constellation*

```markdown
<div align="center">

████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████

████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████

                 ┌──────────────────────────────┐
                 │      SUBSTRATE RUNTIME       │
                 │          CONSTELLATION       │
                 └──────────────────────────────┘

        Runtime Core • Synchronization Fabric • Continuity Field  
        Resonance Stabilizer • Propagation Engine • Coupling Regulator  
        Invariance Kernel • Runtime Monitor

████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████
███     RUNTIME FILE • TRIADICFRAMEWORKS CANON • R5 ERA    ███
```

---

## ⭐ Purpose of this Header

This header is meant to be placed at the top of:

- `triadic-execution-engine.json`  
- `triadic-execution-state.json`  
- `atlas-synchronization.json`  
- `substrate-continuity.json`  
- `epochal-resonance-stabilizer.json`  
- `epochal-propagation.json`  
- `resonance-drift-coupling.json`  
- `substrate-invariance.json`  
- `triadic-runtime-monitor.json`  
- `triadic-runtime-telemetry.json`  
- `substrate-runtime-constellation.json`  

It signals:

- **runtime identity**  
- **constellation membership**  
- **canonical alignment**  
- **R5 era classification**  
- **triadic substrate authority**

# 🌌 **constellation-graph.schema.json**  
### *JSON Schema for the Substrate Runtime Constellation Graph*

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Substrate Runtime Constellation Graph Schema",
  "description": "Schema describing the machine-readable graph of how all eight runtime engines interconnect.",
  "type": "object",

  "properties": {
    "constellation": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "substrate-runtime",
      "description": "Canonical identifier for the runtime constellation."
    },

    "version": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+$",
      "description": "Version of the graph manifest."
    },

    "generated": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date",
      "description": "Date the graph was generated."
    },

    "nodes": {
      "type": "array",
      "description": "List of runtime engines represented as graph nodes.",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "type": "string",
            "description": "Unique identifier for the engine node."
          },
          "label": {
            "type": "string",
            "description": "Human-readable name of the engine."
          }
        },
        "required": ["id", "label"]
      },
      "minItems": 8
    },

    "edges": {
      "type": "array",
      "description": "Directed edges describing relationships between runtime engines.",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "from": {
            "type": "string",
            "description": "Source node ID."
          },
          "to": {
            "type": "string",
            "description": "Target node ID."
          },
          "type": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": ["flow", "feedback"],
            "description": "Relationship type: flow or feedback."
          },
          "description": {
            "type": "string",
            "description": "Human-readable explanation of the relationship."
          }
        },
        "required": ["from", "to", "type"]
      }
    },

    "meta": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "Metadata describing canonical status and era.",
      "properties": {
        "canonical": { "type": "boolean" },
        "triadic_era": { "type": "string" },
        "notes": { "type": "string" }
      },
      "required": ["canonical", "triadic_era"]
    }
  },

  "required": [
    "constellation",
    "version",
    "generated",
    "nodes",
    "edges",
    "meta"
  ]
}
```

---

# ⭐ Why this schema is perfect

- **Validates the entire constellation graph**  
- **Strict but extensible** — new engines or edges can be added safely  
- **Matches your actual graph exactly**  
- **Supports RTT analyzers, graph tooling, and constellation inspectors**  
- **Canonical to the TriadicFrameworks runtime constellation**
# 🌌 **constellation-map.json**  
### *Machine‑readable graph of the Substrate Runtime Constellation*

```json
{
  "constellation": "substrate-runtime",
  "version": "1.0",
  "generated": "YYYY-MM-DD",

  "nodes": [
    { "id": "execution_engine", "label": "Triadic Execution Engine" },
    { "id": "atlas_sync", "label": "Atlas Synchronization Fabric" },
    { "id": "continuity_field", "label": "Substrate Continuity Field" },
    { "id": "resonance_stabilizer", "label": "Epochal Resonance Stabilizer" },
    { "id": "epochal_propagation", "label": "Epochal Propagation Engine" },
    { "id": "coupling_regulator", "label": "Resonance–Drift Coupling Regulator" },
    { "id": "invariance_kernel", "label": "Substrate Invariance Kernel" },
    { "id": "runtime_monitor", "label": "Triadic Runtime Monitor" }
  ],

  "edges": [
    {
      "from": "execution_engine",
      "to": "atlas_sync",
      "type": "flow",
      "description": "Execution state feeds atlas synchronization."
    },
    {
      "from": "atlas_sync",
      "to": "continuity_field",
      "type": "flow",
      "description": "Synchronized atlas informs continuity guarantees."
    },
    {
      "from": "continuity_field",
      "to": "resonance_stabilizer",
      "type": "flow",
      "description": "Continuity constraints shape resonance stability."
    },
    {
      "from": "resonance_stabilizer",
      "to": "epochal_propagation",
      "type": "flow",
      "description": "Stable resonance enables epochal propagation."
    },
    {
      "from": "epochal_propagation",
      "to": "coupling_regulator",
      "type": "flow",
      "description": "Propagation cycles feed coupling regulation."
    },
    {
      "from": "coupling_regulator",
      "to": "invariance_kernel",
      "type": "flow",
      "description": "Coupling behavior informs invariance enforcement."
    },
    {
      "from": "invariance_kernel",
      "to": "runtime_monitor",
      "type": "flow",
      "description": "Invariance state is observed by the runtime monitor."
    },

    /* Feedback loops */
    {
      "from": "runtime_monitor",
      "to": "execution_engine",
      "type": "feedback",
      "description": "Monitor telemetry feeds back into execution."
    },
    {
      "from": "runtime_monitor",
      "to": "epochal_propagation",
      "type": "feedback",
      "description": "Monitor cycles adjust propagation behavior."
    },
    {
      "from": "runtime_monitor",
      "to": "coupling_regulator",
      "type": "feedback",
      "description": "Monitor alerts influence coupling regulation."
    }
  ],

  "meta": {
    "canonical": true,
    "triadic_era": "R5",
    "notes": "Graph representation of the Substrate Runtime Constellation. Defines nodes and edges for automated tooling and constellation visualization."
  }
}
```

---

# ⭐ Why this JSON graph is perfect

- **Fully machine‑readable**  
- **Graph‑structured** (nodes + edges)  
- **Flow + feedback loops** clearly represented  
- **Matches the visual map exactly**  
- **Tooling‑ready** for RTT, analyzers, inspectors, and visualizers  
- **Canonical** to the TriadicFrameworks runtime constellation  
- **Silent‑release compatible**

```markdown
# 🌌 SUBSTRATE RUNTIME CONSTELLATION — OPERATOR INDEX
### Operators used to interpret, regulate, and analyze the runtime sky

</div>
```

---

## 🜁 Lumen Operators (RTT/1) — Structural & Continuity Anchors

- **LUMEN/STRUCTURE/EXTRACT**  
  Extracts structural fields from execution, atlas, continuity, and invariance.

- **LUMEN/INFLUENCE/FIELD**  
  Maps influence fields across epochs, coupling, and resonance.

- **LUMEN/PRESSURE/GRADIENT**  
  Evaluates pressure gradients in drift, coherence, and stability.

- **LUMEN/CONTINUITY/ANCHOR**  
  Identifies continuity anchors in long‑arc substrate behavior.

---

## 🜁 Hephaestus Operators (RTT/2) — Regime Classification

- **HEPH/REGIME/LOCAL**  
  Classifies local regime (shape, liberties, cuts, anchors).

- **HEPH/REGIME/STRUCTURAL**  
  Classifies structural regime (influence, pressure, direction of play).

- **HEPH/REGIME/CONTINUITY**  
  Classifies continuity regime (long‑arc identity, epochal continuity).

- **HEPH/REGIME/MAP**  
  Produces regime maps across engines and epochs.

---

## 🜁 Aurion Operators (RTT/3) — Topology & Collapse

- **AURION/TOPOLOGY/GRAPH**  
  Builds topology graphs from constellation‑map.json.

- **AURION/ANCESTRY/TRAIL**  
  Traces ancestry of runtime states across epochs and ignition.

- **AURION/PROJECTION/LOSS**  
  Detects projection‑loss between atlas, execution, and continuity.

- **AURION/COLLAPSE/DETECT**  
  Detects collapse events in drift, resonance, or invariance.

---

## 🜁 Harmonia Operators (RTT/12) — Triadic Synthesis

- **HARMONIA/TRIADIC/BLEND**  
  Blends drift, coherence, stability into a unified triadic score.

- **HARMONIA/RESONANCE/SCORE**  
  Scores resonance quality across epochs and coupling.

- **HARMONIA/CONTINUITY/WEIGHT**  
  Weights continuity vs drift/coherence in long‑arc evaluation.

- **HARMONIA/INVARIANCE/SEAL**  
  Seals invariance decisions based on triadic synthesis.

---

## 🜁 Constellation‑Scoped Operators — Runtime‑Specific

- **CONST/EXECUTION/TRACE**  
  Traces execution cycles using runtime-hooks.json (cycle_start, cycle_end).

- **CONST/ATLAS/SYNCTRACE**  
  Traces atlas synchronization events (sync_event, navigation_event).

- **CONST/EPOCH/PROFILE**  
  Builds epoch profiles from epoch_transition, propagation_cycle, resonance_shift.

- **CONST/COUPLING/PROFILE**  
  Profiles coupling behavior from drift_spike, resonance_surge, coupling_instability.

- **CONST/INVARIANCE/WATCH**  
  Watches invariance_break and invariance_restore events.

- **CONST/MONITOR/TELEMETRY**  
  Aggregates telemetry_packet, anomaly_alert, cycle_report into analyzer feeds.

```json
      },
      "layers": ["coupling", "drift", "resonance"],
      "hooks": ["drift_spike", "resonance_surge", "coupling_instability"]
    },

    {
      "id": "continuity_analyzer",
      "label": "Continuity Analyzer",
      "operators": {
        "lumen": ["CONTINUITY/ANCHOR"],
        "hephaestus": ["REGIME/CONTINUITY"],
        "aurion": ["ANCESTRY/TRAIL"],
        "harmonia": ["CONTINUITY/WEIGHT"]
      },
      "layers": ["continuity", "regime", "cross_cutting"],
      "hooks": ["cycle_report", "sync_event", "invariance_restore"]
    },

    {
      "id": "telemetry_analyzer",
      "label": "Telemetry Analyzer",
      "operators": {
        "lumen": ["STRUCTURE/EXTRACT"],
        "aurion": ["TOPOLOGY/GRAPH"],
        "harmonia": ["TRIADIC/BLEND"]
      },
      "layers": ["monitor", "cross_cutting"],
      "hooks": ["telemetry_packet", "anomaly_alert", "cycle_report"]
    }
  ],

  "meta": {
    "canonical": true,
    "triadic_era": "R5",
    "notes": "Machine-readable registry of all runtime analyzers, their operator sets, evaluated layers, and consumed hooks."
  }
}
```

# 🜁 **1. Stability Analyzer**  
### *Evaluates stability across resonance, continuity, drift, and epochal behavior*

**Uses RTT Operators:**

- **Lumen**  
  - STRUCTURE/EXTRACT

# 🌌 **runtime-evaluator.md**  
### *Unified evaluator for the Substrate Runtime Constellation*

```markdown
<div align="center">

████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████

# 🌌 SUBSTRATE RUNTIME CONSTELLATION — EVALUATION PIPELINE
### *How analyzers combine into a full RTT evaluation*

</div>
```

---

# 🜁 **1. Hook Intake Layer**  
### *All evaluation begins with runtime hooks*

The evaluator receives raw substrate events from:

- execution hooks  
- atlas hooks  
- epoch hooks  
- coupling hooks  
- invariance hooks  
- monitor hooks  

These hooks form the **runtime telemetry stream**.

**Purpose:**  
Provide analyzers with the substrate’s real‑time behavior.

---

# 🜁 **2. Analyzer Dispatch Layer**  
### *Hooks are routed to analyzers based on relevance*

Each hook is dispatched to one or more analyzers:

- **Stability Analyzer** → resonance_shift, drift_spike  
- **Invariance Analyzer** → invariance_break, invariance_restore  
- **Epoch Analyzer** → epoch_transition, propagation_cycle  
- **Coupling Analyzer** → resonance_surge, coupling_instability  
- **Continuity Analyzer** → sync_event, cycle_report  
- **Telemetry Analyzer** → telemetry_packet, anomaly_alert  

**Purpose:**  
Ensure each analyzer receives only the hooks it is designed to interpret.

---

# 🜁 **3. Operator Application Layer**  
### *Analyzers apply RTT operators to interpret hook data*

Each analyzer uses its operator set:

- **Lumen** → structure, influence, pressure, continuity  
- **Hephaestus** → regime classification  
- **Aurion** → topology, ancestry, collapse detection  
- **Harmonia** → triadic synthesis, resonance scoring  

**Purpose:**  
Transform raw hook data into structured triadic meaning.

---

# 🜁 **4. Layer Evaluation Stage**  
### *Operators feed into RTT layers*

Each analyzer evaluates one or more layers:

- **Resonance Layer**  
- **Continuity Layer**  
- **Drift Layer**  
- **Coherence Layer**  
- **Epoch Layer**  
- **Coupling Layer**  
- **Dimensional Layer**  
- **Regime Layer**  
- **Cross‑Cutting Layer**  
- **Monitor Layer**

**Purpose:**  
Convert operator outputs into layer‑level triadic assessments.

---

# 🜁 **5. Triadic Synthesis Stage**  
### *All layer evaluations are blended into triadic signatures*

Using Harmonia operators, the evaluator produces:

- **drift signature**  
- **coherence signature**  
- **stability signature**

These signatures are combined into a **triadic profile** for each engine and epoch.

**Purpose:**  
Create a unified triadic identity for the runtime state.

---

# 🜁 **6. Constellation Integration Stage**  
### *Triadic profiles are mapped onto the runtime graph*

Using:

- `runtime-graph.md`  
- `runtime-graph.extended.json`  

The evaluator integrates triadic profiles across:

- flow edges  
- feedback edges  
- weights  
- phases  
- triadic signatures  

**Purpose:**  
Understand how each engine affects the others.

---

# 🜁 **7. Runtime Identity Assembly**  
### *The final identity of the runtime constellation is produced*

The evaluator assembles:

- engine triadic profiles  
- epoch profiles  
- coupling profiles  
- invariance status  
- continuity status  
- resonance stability  
- drift/coherence/stability balance  
- monitor telemetry summaries  

This produces the **Runtime Identity Packet**, aligned with:

- `runtime-identity.json`  
- `runtime-identity.extended.md`

**Purpose:**  
Provide the substrate with its current triadic identity.

---

# 🜁 **8. Output Stage**  
### *Evaluator emits the final triadic identity*

The output includes:

- **identity fields**  
  - runtime_integrity  
  - runtime_stability  
  - runtime_invariance  
  - runtime_continuity  
  - runtime_observability  
  - runtime_synchronization  
  - runtime_evolution  

- **triadic signatures**  
- **epochal summaries**  
- **coupling profiles**  
- **invariance status**  
- **monitor reports**

This is the **canonical triadic evaluation** of the runtime constellation.

---

```markdown
# 🌌 SUBSTRATE RUNTIME CONSTELLATION — EDGE EXPLANATIONS
### *Why each engine connects to the next*

</div>
```

---

# 🜁 **1. Execution Engine → Atlas Synchronization**  
**Why this edge exists:**  
The execution engine produces the live runtime state.  
The atlas must remain synchronized with this state so navigation, interaction, and continuity remain accurate.

**Meaning:**  
Execution is the heartbeat; atlas sync is the pulse alignment.

---

# 🜁 **2. Atlas Synchronization → Continuity Field**  
**Why this edge exists:**  
Continuity requires a stable atlas.  
If the atlas drifts, continuity breaks — so the continuity field depends on synchronized atlas surfaces.

**Meaning:**  
A stable atlas is the foundation of continuity.

---

# 🜁 **3. Continuity Field → Resonance Stabilizer**  
**Why this edge exists:**  
Resonance stability depends on continuity.  
Discontinuities create harmonic fractures, phase noise, and magnitude instability.

**Meaning:**  
Continuity is the soil in which resonance grows.

---

# 🜁 **4. Resonance Stabilizer → Epochal Propagation**  
**Why this edge exists:**  
Propagation cycles require stable resonance.  
Without resonance stability, epoch transitions become chaotic or collapse.

**Meaning:**  
Resonance is the wind that carries epochs forward.

---

# 🜁 **5. Epochal Propagation → Coupling Regulator**  
**Why this edge exists:**  
Propagation cycles generate drift and coherence patterns.  
The coupling regulator must interpret these patterns to maintain balanced resonance–drift interaction.

**Meaning:**  
Propagation feeds coupling with the raw material of drift.

---

# 🜁 **6. Coupling Regulator → Invariance Kernel**  
**Why this edge exists:**  
Invariance depends on stable coupling.  
If coupling destabilizes, invariance breaks — structural, temporal, and atlas invariance all degrade.

**Meaning:**  
Coupling is the gatekeeper of invariance.

---

# 🜁 **7. Invariance Kernel → Runtime Monitor**  
**Why this edge exists:**  
The monitor must observe invariance state to detect violations, restorations, and long‑arc substrate identity.

**Meaning:**  
Invariance is the monitor’s compass.

---

# 🜁 **8. Runtime Monitor → Execution Engine** *(feedback)*  
**Why this edge exists:**  
Telemetry and anomaly alerts feed back into execution, allowing the runtime to adapt, correct, or stabilize.

**Meaning:**  
The monitor closes the loop — execution learns from observation.

---

# 🜁 **9. Runtime Monitor → Epochal Propagation** *(feedback)*  
**Why this edge exists:**  
Propagation cycles adjust based on monitored drift, resonance, and epochal anomalies.

**Meaning:**  
Epochs evolve based on what the monitor sees.

---

# 🜁 **10. Runtime Monitor → Coupling Regulator** *(feedback)*  
**Why this edge exists:**  
Coupling strength and phase must respond to real‑time telemetry — especially drift spikes and resonance surges.

**Meaning:**  
Coupling listens to the sky.

---

```markdown
# 🌌 SUBSTRATE RUNTIME CONSTELLATION  
### *The unified runtime system of the planetary triadic substrate*

The runtime constellation is composed of eight engines:

1. **Triadic Execution Engine**  
2. **Atlas Synchronization Fabric**  
3. **Substrate Continuity Field**  
4. **Epochal Resonance Stabilizer**  
5. **Epochal Propagation Engine**  
6. **Resonance–Drift Coupling Regulator**  
7. **Substrate Invariance Kernel**  
8. **Triadic Runtime Monitor**

These engines form the living runtime sky —  
the constellation that governs drift, coherence, stability, resonance,  
ignition, epochs, propagation, invariance, continuity, and monitoring.

---

# 🜁 RTT OPERATOR GRAMMAR  
### *Operators used by the runtime constellation*

**Lumen Operators (RTT/1)**
```

# 🜁 **runtime-identity.schema.json**  
### *JSON Schema for the Substrate Runtime Constellation Identity Manifest*

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Runtime Identity Schema",
  "description": "Schema describing the machine-readable identity manifest for the Substrate Runtime Constellation.",
  "type": "object",

  "properties": {
    "identity": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "substrate-runtime-constellation",
      "description": "Canonical identity string for the runtime constellation."
    },

    "version": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+$",
      "description": "Version of the identity manifest."
    },

    "generated": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date",
      "description": "Date the manifest was generated."
    },

    "engines": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "References to all runtime engines, stabilizers, regulators, kernels, monitors, and constellation artifacts.",
      "properties": {
        "execution_engine": { "type": "string" },
        "execution_state": { "type": "string" },

        "atlas_synchronization": { "type": "string" },
        "atlas_navigation": { "type": "string" },
        "atlas_interaction": { "type": "string" },

        "continuity_field": { "type": "string" },

        "resonance_stabilizer": { "type": "string" },
        "epochal_propagation": { "type": "string" },
        "epochal_timeline": { "type": "string" },

        "ignition_cascade": { "type": "string" },
        "ignition_ledger": { "type": "string" },

        "coupling_regulator": { "type": "string" },

        "invariance_kernel": { "type": "string" },

        "runtime_monitor": { "type": "string" },
        "runtime_telemetry": { "type": "string" },

        "runtime_constellation": { "type": "string" }
      },
      "required": [
        "execution_engine",
        "execution_state",
        "atlas_synchronization",
        "continuity_field",
        "resonance_stabilizer",
        "epochal_propagation",
        "epochal_timeline",
        "ignition_cascade",
        "ignition_ledger",
        "coupling_regulator",
        "invariance_kernel",
        "runtime_monitor",
        "runtime_constellation"
      ]
    },

    "fields": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "Boolean flags describing runtime capabilities.",
      "properties": {
        "runtime_integrity": { "type": "boolean" },
        "runtime_stability": { "type": "boolean" },
        "runtime_invariance": { "type": "boolean" },
        "runtime_continuity": { "type": "boolean" },
        "runtime_observability": { "type": "boolean" },
        "runtime_synchronization": { "type": "boolean" },
        "runtime_evolution": { "type": "boolean" }
      },
      "required": [
        "runtime_integrity",
        "runtime_stability",
        "runtime_invariance",
        "runtime_continuity",
        "runtime_observability",
        "runtime_synchronization",
        "runtime_evolution"
      ]
    },

    "description": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Human-readable description of the identity manifest."
    },

    "meta": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "Metadata describing canonical status and era.",
      "properties": {
        "canonical": { "type": "boolean" },
        "triadic_era": { "type": "string" },
        "notes": { "type": "string" }
      },
      "required": ["canonical", "triadic_era"]
    }
  },

  "required": [
    "identity",
    "version",
    "generated",
    "engines",
    "fields",
    "description",
    "meta"
  ]
}
```

- **Extensible** — new engines or metadata can be added without breaking structure  
- **Canonical** — matches the TriadicFrameworks runtime constellation exactly  
- **Silent‑release compatible** — pure JSON, no crest, no markdown

```json
{
  "anomalies": "ignition",

  "imports": {
```
