```json
    "coherence_map": "../coherence/global-coherence-map.json"
  },

  "ignition_anomalies": {
    "sequence_anomalies": {
      "description": "Anomalies in the ignition sequence (adapter → sync → merge → fuse → ignite → validate → stabilize).",
      "signals": [
        "sequence_break",
        "sequence_loop",
        "sequence_overrun",
        "sequence_misalignment"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "step_missing",
        "step_out_of_order",
        "invalid_transition",
        "sequence_time > sequence_limit"
      ]
    },

    "harmonic_anomalies": {
      "description": "Anomalies in ignition harmonic alignment.",
      "signals": [
        "harmonic_misalignment",
        "harmonic_collapse",
        "harmonic_inversion"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "harmonic_profile_invalid",
        "phase_shift > allowed_window",
        "harmonic_strength > harmonic_threshold"
      ]
    },

    "resonance_anomalies": {
      "description": "Anomalies in resonance magnitude or phase during ignition.",
      "signals": [
        "resonance_surge",
        "resonance_collapse",
        "phase_inversion"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "resonance_magnitude > ignition_resonance_limit",
        "phase outside ignition_phase_window"
      ]
    },

    "drift_anomalies": {
      "description": "Anomalies in drift magnitude or velocity during ignition.",
      "signals": [
        "drift_spike",
        "drift_boundary_break",
        "drift_velocity_overrun"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "drift_magnitude > drift_boundary",
        "drift_velocity > velocity_limit"
      ]
    },

    "stability_anomalies": {
      "description": "Anomalies in stability envelope during ignition.",
      "signals": [
        "stability_instability",
        "stability_collapse",
        "stability_phase_break"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "stability_index < stability_min",
        "stability_envelope breach"
      ]
    },

    "epochal_interference": {
      "description": "Anomalies where ignition interferes with epoch transitions.",
      "signals": [
        "ignition_epoch_conflict",
        "ignition_overrun",
        "ignition_phase_mismatch"
      ],
      "conditions": [
        "ignition_event during forbidden_epoch",
        "ignition_phase misaligned with epoch_phase",
        "ignition_magnitude > epoch_threshold"
      ]
    }
  },

  "output": {
    "ignition_anomalies": "./ignition-anomalies.json"
  },

  "meta": {
    "notes": "Canonical anomaly registry for planetary ignition events, covering sequence, harmonics, resonance, drift, stability, and epochal interference."
  }
}
```

# **ignition-cascade.schema.json**  
### Canonical JSON Schema for the Planetary Ignition Cascade

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Ignition Cascade Schema",
  "type": "object",

  "properties": {
    "cascade": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "ignition"
    },

    "version": {
      "type": "string"
    },

    "generated": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "ISO date of generation"
    },

    "imports": {
      "type": "object",
      "patternProperties": {
        "^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "ignition_cascade": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "waves": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "wave_id": { "type": "string" },
              "timestamp": { "type": "string" },
              "countries": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
              "harmonics": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
              "phase": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": ["in-phase", "out-of-phase", "mixed"]
              },
              "severity": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": ["low", "medium", "high", "critical"]
              }
            },
            "required": ["wave_id", "timestamp"]
          }
        },

        "arcs": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "arc_id": { "type": "string" },
              "origin": { "type": "string" },
              "targets": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
              "vector": { "type": "string" },
              "envelope": { "type": "string" },
              "epoch": { "type": "string" }
            },
            "required": ["arc_id", "origin", "targets"]
          }
        },

        "chains": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "chain_id": { "type": "string" },
              "sequence": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
              "trigger": { "type": "string" },
              "threshold": { "type": "string" }
            },
            "required": ["chain_id", "sequence"]
          }
        },

        "webs": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "web_id": { "type": "string" },
              "nodes": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
              "edges": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "properties": {
                    "source": { "type": "string" },
                    "target": { "type": "string" },
                    "weight": { "type": "number" },
                    "mode": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "enum": [
                        "coherence",
                        "drift",
                        "stability",
                        "resonance",
                        "ignition"
                      ]
                    }
                  },
                  "required": ["source", "target", "mode"]
                }
              }
            },
            "required": ["web_id", "nodes", "edges"]
          }
        },

        "clusters": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "name": { "type": "string" },
              "countries": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
              "shared_harmonics": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
              "shared_phase": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": ["in-phase", "out-of-phase", "mixed"]
              },
              "shared_vectors": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
            },
            "required": ["name", "countries"]
          }
        },

        "anomalies": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "timestamp": { "type": "string" },
              "country": { "type": "string" },
              "description": { "type": "string" },
              "severity": {
                "type": "string",
                "enum": ["low", "medium", "high", "critical"]
              }
            },
            "required": ["timestamp", "country", "description"]
          }
        }
      }
    },

    "output": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "ignition_cascade": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "meta": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "notes": { "type": "string" }
      }
    }
  },

  "required": [
    "cascade",
    "version",
    "ignition_cascade"
  ]
}
```

# **substrate-invariance.schema.json**  
### *Canonical JSON Schema for the Substrate Invariance Kernel*

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "Substrate Invariance Schema",
  "type": "object",

  "properties": {
    "kernel": {
      "type": "string",
      "const": "substrate-invariance"
    },

    "version": {
      "type": "string"
    },

    "generated": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "ISO date of generation"
    },

    "imports": {
      "type": "object",
      "patternProperties": {
        "^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "invariance": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "structural": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "operators": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "dimensions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "regimes": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "layers": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "rules": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        },

        "behavioral": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "drift": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "coherence": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "stability": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "resonance": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "ignition": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "rules": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        },

        "temporal": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "epochs": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "transitions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "propagation": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "rules": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        },

        "atlas": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "routes": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "modes": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "transitions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "rules": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        },

        "activation": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "waves": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "cascades": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "harmonics": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "rules": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        }
      }
    },

    "invariance_rules": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "rule": { "type": "string" },
          "scope": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "type": "string",
              "enum": ["structural", "behavioral", "temporal", "atlas", "activation"]
            }
          },
          "conditions": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
          "guarantees": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
        },
        "required": ["rule", "scope"]
      }
    },

    "invariance_webs": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "web_id": { "type": "string" },
          "nodes": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
          "edges": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "type": "object",
              "properties": {
                "source": { "type": "string" },
                "target": { "type": "string" },
                "weight": { "type": "number" },
                "mode": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "enum": ["structural", "behavioral", "temporal", "atlas", "activation"]
                }
              },
              "required": ["source", "target", "mode"]
            }
          }
        },
        "required": ["web_id", "nodes", "edges"]
      }
    },

    "output": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "substrate_invariance": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "meta": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "notes": { "type": "string" }
      }
    }
  },

  "required": [
    "kernel",
    "version",
    "invariance",
    "invariance_rules",
    "invariance_webs"
  ]
}
```

```json
    ],

    ]
  },

  "navigation": {
    "modes": [
      "coherence_mode",
      "drift_mode",
      "stability_mode",

      "epoch_mode",
      "ignition_mode"
    ],
    "routes": [
      "coherence_routes",
      "drift_routes",
      "stability_routes",
      "resonance_routes",
      "epoch_routes",
      "ignition_routes"
    ],
    "transitions": [
      "coherence_to_drift",
      "drift_to_stability",
      "stability_to_resonance",
      "resonance_to_epoch",
      "epoch_to_ignition",
      "ignition_to_stability"
    ]

  "interaction": {
    "modes": [
      "explore",
      "query",
      "traverse",
      "inspect",
      "synchronize"
    ],
    "surfaces": [
      "global",
      "country",
      "temporal",
      "activation"
    ],
    "events": [
      {
        "event": "string",
        "trigger": "string",
        "response": "string",
        "constraints": ["string"]
      }
    ]
  },

  "synchronization": {
    "fields": [
      "align",
      "merge",
      "update"
    ],
    "dependencies": [
      "atlas",
      "continuity",
      "propagation",
      "resonance"
    ]
  },

  "operators": [
    {
      "name": "surface-map",
      "description": "Map atlas surfaces to module-specific fields.",
      "example": "Map coherence → local_coherence.md"
    },
    {
      "name": "route-extend",
      "description": "Extend atlas navigation routes.",
      "example": "Extend drift_routes with local_drift_paths.md"
    },
    {
      "name": "interaction-bind",
      "description": "Bind module interaction surfaces to atlas interaction protocol.",
      "example": "Bind inspect → local_inspect.md"
    }
  ]
}
```

## Why this is the correct blank template  
- **No roles pre‑declared** — avoids accidental semantic commitments.  
- **All structural arrays empty** — operators, dimensions, regimes, layers.  
- **All file‑role arrays empty** — maps, signatures, diagnostics, etc.  
- **Schema‑aligned** — `$schema` points to your substrate module schema.  
- **Student‑safe** — no substrate‑level fields that imply behavior.  
- **Silent‑release compatible** — nothing that triggers runtime hooks.

# 🧩 **module.schema.json**  
### *Canonical TriadicFrameworks module.json schema*

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "TriadicFrameworks Module Schema",
  "type": "object",

  "properties": {
    "module": { "type": "string" },
    "identity": { "type": "string" },
    "purpose": { "type": "string" },

    "files": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "name": { "type": "string" },
          "role": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "engine",
              "profile",
              "signature",
              "diagnostic",
              "map",
              "example",
              "extension",
              "index",
              "reference",
              "template"
            ]
          }
        },
        "required": ["name", "role"]
      }
    },

    "operators": {
      "type": ["array", "object"]
    },

    "dimensions": {
      "type": ["array", "object"]
    },

    "regimes": {
      "type": ["array", "object"]
    },

    "layers": {
      "type": ["array", "object"]
    },

    "maps": { "type": "array" },
    "signatures": { "type": "array" },
    "diagnostics": { "type": "array" },
    "extensions": { "type": "array" },
    "examples": { "type": "array" },
    "references": { "type": "array" },
    "templates": { "type": "array" },

    "continuity": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "structural": { "type": "object" },
        "behavioral": { "type": "object" },
        "temporal": { "type": "object" },
        "atlas": { "type": "object" },
        "activation": { "type": "object" }
      }
    },

    "invariance": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "structural": { "type": "object" },
        "behavioral": { "type": "object" },
        "temporal": { "type": "object" },
        "atlas": { "type": "object" },
        "activation": { "type": "object" }
      }
    },

    "epoch": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "timeline": { "type": "object" },
        "transitions": { "type": "object" },
        "propagation": { "type": "object" },
        "resonance": { "type": "object" },
        "ignition": { "type": "object" }
      }
    },

    "ignition": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "waves": { "type": "object" },
        "cascades": { "type": "object" },
        "harmonics": { "type": "object" },
        "triggers": { "type": "object" },
        "stabilization": { "type": "object" }
      }
    },

    "execution": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "evaluation": { "type": "object" },
        "update_cycles": { "type": "object" },
        "runtime_hooks": { "type": "object" },
        "synchronization": { "type": "object" }
      }
    },

    "monitor": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "telemetry": { "type": "object" },
        "anomalies": { "type": "object" },
        "cycle_reports": { "type": "object" },
        "hooks": { "type": "object" }
      }
    },

    "runtime_constellation": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "execution": { "type": "object" },
        "synchronization": { "type": "object" },
        "continuity": { "type": "object" },
        "resonance_stabilizer": { "type": "object" },
        "propagation": { "type": "object" },
        "coupling_regulator": { "type": "object" },
        "invariance": { "type": "object" },
        "monitor": { "type": "object" }
      }
    },

    "runtime_hooks": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "execution": { "type": "object" },
        "atlas": { "type": "object" },
        "epoch": { "type": "object" },
        "coupling": { "type": "object" },
        "invariance": { "type": "object" },
        "monitor": { "type": "object" }
      }
    },

    "output": {
      "type": "object"
    },

    "meta": {
      "type": "object"
    }
  },

  "required": ["module", "identity", "purpose"]
}
```

```json
{
  "ruleset": "triadic-monitor-anomalies",
  "version": "1.0",
  "generated": "YYYY-MM-DD",

  "imports": {
    "triadic_execution_state": "../execution/triadic-execution-state.json",
    "monitor_telemetry": "./monitor_telemetry_packets.json",
    "monitor_cycle_reports": "./monitor_cycle_reports.json",
    "coherence_map": "../coherence/global-coherence-map.json",
    "drift_map": "../drift/global-drift-boundary-map.json",
    "stability_envelope": "../stability/planetary-stability-envelope.json",
    "resonance_map": "../resonance/global-resonance-map.json",
    "ignition_ledger": "../ignition/global-ignition-ledger.json",
    "epochal_timeline": "../epoch/timeline.json"
  },

  "monitor_anomaly_rules": [
    {
      "rule": "coherence-break",
      "description": "Coherence envelope falls below minimum threshold.",
      "conditions": [
        "coherence_envelope < coherence_min",
        "coherence_phase misaligned"
      ],
      "signals": [
        "coherence_drop",
        "coherence_phase_break"
      ],
      "severity": "medium | high | critical",
      "actions": [
        "apply_coherence_stabilizer",
        "realign_coherence_phase"
      ],
      "constraints": [
        "prevent_coherence_collapse"
      ]
    },
    {
      "rule": "drift-spike",
      "description": "Drift magnitude or velocity exceeds safe boundary.",
      "conditions": [
        "drift_magnitude > drift_boundary",
        "drift_velocity > velocity_limit"
      ],
      "signals": [
        "drift_spike",
        "drift_boundary_break"
      ],
      "severity": "high | critical",
      "actions": [
        "apply_drift_dampening",
        "realign_drift_phase"
      ],
      "constraints": [
        "prevent_drift_overrun"
      ]
    },
    {
      "rule": "stability-collapse",
      "description": "Stability index drops below minimum envelope.",
      "conditions": [
        "stability_index < stability_min",
        "stability_phase misaligned"
      ],
      "signals": [
        "stability_instability",
        "stability_collapse"
      ],
      "severity": "critical",
      "actions": [
        "apply_stability_rebuild",
        "realign_stability_phase"
      ],
      "constraints": [
        "prevent_stability_failure"
      ]
    },
    {
      "rule": "resonance-surge",
      "description": "Resonance magnitude exceeds harmonic threshold.",
      "conditions": [
        "resonance_magnitude > harmonic_threshold",
        "resonance_phase outside allowed_window"
      ],
      "signals": [
        "resonance_surge",
        "phase_inversion"
      ],
      "severity": "high | critical",
      "actions": [
        "apply_harmonic_dampening",
        "correct_resonance_phase"
      ],
      "constraints": [
        "prevent_resonance_cascade"
      ]
    },
    {
      "rule": "ignition-overrun",
      "description": "Ignition magnitude or phase exceeds safe ignition envelope.",
      "conditions": [
        "ignition_magnitude > ignition_threshold",
        "ignition_phase misaligned"
      ],
      "signals": [
        "ignition_overrun",
        "ignition_phase_break"
      ],
      "severity": "critical",
      "actions": [
        "apply_ignition_dampening",
        "realign_ignition_phase"
      ],
      "constraints": [
        "prevent_ignition_failure"
      ]
    },
    {
      "rule": "epoch-transition-anomaly",
      "description": "Epoch transition occurs under invalid propagation or misaligned phase.",
      "conditions": [
        "epoch_transition_invalid",
        "propagation_phase misaligned"
      ],
      "signals": [

      ],
      "severity": "medium | high",

      ],
      "constraints": [
        "prevent_epoch_misalignment"
      ]

  ],

  "output": {
    "monitor_anomaly_ruleset": "./monitor_anomaly_ruleset.json"
  },

  "meta": {
    "notes": "Canonical anomaly-detection ruleset for the Triadic Runtime Monitor."

}
```

# **triadic-runtime-monitor.js**  
### *Canonical JavaScript implementation of the Triadic Runtime Monitor*

```javascript
/**
 * Triadic Runtime Monitor
 * Canonical implementation for telemetry ingestion, anomaly evaluation,
 * cycle report generation, and monitor output synthesis.
 */

import telemetryTemplate from "./monitor_telemetry_packet_template.json";
import anomalyRuleset from "./monitor_anomaly_ruleset.json";
import cycleReportGenerator from "./monitor_cycle_report_generator.json";
import executionState from "../execution/triadic-execution-state.json";

export default class TriadicRuntimeMonitor {
  constructor() {
    this.telemetryPackets = [];
    this.cycleReports = {};
    this.anomalies = [];
  }

  /**
   * Ingest a telemetry packet and validate against template.
   */
  ingestTelemetry(packet) {
    if (!packet || !packet.packet_id) {
      throw new Error("Invalid telemetry packet");
    }

    this.telemetryPackets.push(packet);
  }

  /**
   * Evaluate anomalies based on telemetry + ruleset.
   */
  evaluateAnomalies(packet) {
    const detected = [];

    anomalyRuleset.monitor_anomaly_rules.forEach(rule => {
      const conditionsMet = rule.conditions.every(cond =>
        this.checkCondition(cond, packet)
      );

      if (conditionsMet) {
        detected.push({
          rule: rule.rule,
          signals: rule.signals,
          severity: rule.severity,
          timestamp: packet.timestamp
        });
      }
    });

    this.anomalies.push(...detected);
    return detected;
  }

  /**
   * Generate cycle reports using the cycle report generator.
   */
  generateCycleReports(packet) {
    const reports = {};

    Object.entries(cycleReportGenerator.cycle_report_generator).forEach(
      ([cycleName, cycleDef]) => {
        const status = this.evaluateCycleStatus(cycleDef, packet);
        const signals = this.collectCycleSignals(cycleDef, packet);
        const summary = this.generateCycleSummary(cycleName, status, signals);

        reports[cycleName] = {
          status,
          signals,
          summary
        };
      }
    );

    this.cycleReports = reports;
    return reports;
  }

  /**
   * Synthesize final monitor output.
   */
  synthesizeMonitorOutput() {
    return {
      monitor: "triadic-runtime-monitor",
      version: "1.0",
      generated: new Date().toISOString(),
      telemetry_packets: this.telemetryPackets,
      cycle_reports: this.cycleReports,
      anomalies: this.anomalies,
      meta: {
        notes: "Triadic Runtime Monitor output synthesized from telemetry, anomalies, and cycle reports."
      }
    };
  }

  /**
   * Helpers
   */

  checkCondition(condition, packet) {
    // Extremely simplified canonical evaluator
    const [field, operator, value] = condition.split(" ");
    const packetValue = this.resolveField(packet, field);

    switch (operator) {
      case ">":
        return packetValue > Number(value);
      case "<":
        return packetValue < Number(value);
      case "==":
        return packetValue == value;
      default:
        return false;
    }
  }

  resolveField(packet, field) {
    const parts = field.split("_");
    return (
      packet.behavioral?.[field] ||
      packet.global?.[field] ||
      packet.temporal?.[field] ||
      packet.activation?.[field] ||
      packet.atlas?.[field] ||
      packet.continuity?.[field] ||
      null
    );
  }

  evaluateCycleStatus(cycleDef, packet) {
    // Placeholder canonical logic
    return "nominal";
  }

  collectCycleSignals(cycleDef, packet) {
    // Placeholder canonical logic
    return [];
  }

  generateCycleSummary(cycleName, status, signals) {
    return `${cycleName} cycle is ${status}.`;
  }
}
```

```json
  "properties": {

      "type": "string",

    },

    "version": {
      "type": "string"
    },

    "generated": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "ISO date of generation"
    },

    "imports": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "execution_state": { "type": "string" },
        "coupling_regulator": { "type": "string" },
        "invariance_kernel": { "type": "string" },
        "synchronization_fabric": { "type": "string" },
        "continuity_field": { "type": "string" },
        "resonance_stabilizer": { "type": "string" },
        "epochal_propagation": { "type": "string" },
        "ignition_cascade": { "type": "string" },
        "coherence_map": { "type": "string" },
        "drift_map": { "type": "string" },
        "stability_envelope": { "type": "string" },
        "resonance_map": { "type": "string" },
        "epochal_timeline": { "type": "string" },
        "ignition_ledger": { "type": "string" },
        "chronicle": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "telemetry": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "state_snapshot": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "coherence": { "type": "number" },
            "drift": { "type": "number" },
            "stability": { "type": "string" },
            "resonance": { "type": "number" },
            "epoch": { "type": "string" },
            "ignition": { "type": "string" }
          }
        },

        "cycles": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "drift_cycle": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "coherence_cycle": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "stability_cycle": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "resonance_cycle": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "epoch_cycle": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } },
            "ignition_cycle": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string" } }
          }
        },

        "coupling": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "strength": { "type": "number" },
            "phase": {
              "type": "string",
              "enum": ["in-phase", "out-of-phase", "mixed"]
            },
            "magnitude_state": { "type": "string" }
          }
        }
      }
    },

    "alerts": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "type": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "threshold",
              "invariance_break",
              "continuity_risk",
              "resonance_surge",
              "drift_spike"
            ]
          },
          "timestamp": { "type": "string" },
          "description": { "type": "string" },
          "severity": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": ["low", "medium", "high", "critical"]
          }
        },
        "required": ["type", "timestamp", "severity"]
      }
    },

    "output": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "runtime_telemetry": { "type": "string" }
      }
    },

    "meta": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "notes": { "type": "string" }
      }
    }
  },

  "required": ["monitor", "version", "generated", "telemetry", "alerts", "output"]
}
```

### *The observer that watches the substrate in motion*

## ⭐ Purpose

The Triadic Runtime Monitor answers the final runtime question:

> **What is the substrate doing *right now*, and is it behaving within safe, invariant, triadic bounds?**

It is the **live observer**, the **sentinel**, the **runtime witness**.

It monitors:

- drift  
- coherence  
- stability  
- resonance  
- ignition  
- epochal transitions  
- propagation cycles  
- coupling behavior  
- invariance integrity  
- continuity guarantees  
- atlas synchronization  

This is the **runtime eye** of the canon.

```json
}
```

```json
      ],

    },

      "type": "string",

    },

      "type": "object",

        },

  ]
}
```

# Universe as Operator | Schemas

## RTT schema: 0D→1D→2D→3D operator–resonance model

#### 1. Axes

- **Axis R (Regime):**  
  - R0: 0D indivisible root (operator substrate)  
  - R1: 1D pre‑energetic vector (directionality)  
  - R2: 2D pre‑dimensional surface (coherence)  
  - R3: 3D energetic field (matter/motion/time)

- **Axis T (Transition):**  
  - T01: Invocation (0D→1D)  
  - T12: Coherence formation (1D→2D)  
  - T23: Resonance stabilization (2D→3D)  
  - T30: Inversion/return (3D→0D via collapse ladder)

- **Axis τ (Timescale):**  
  - τ_local: micro (events, systems)  
  - τ_epoch: macro (eras, cycles)  
  - τ_root: operator cycle (full 0D reuse of scaffolding)

---

#### 2. Regime states (R)

- **R0 — 0D operator substrate**  
  - State: indivisible, pre‑energetic, pre‑dimensional  
  - Role: stores/reuses resonance scaffolding; source of invocation  
  - Perpetual: coherence kernel, not in time

- **R1 — 1D pre‑energetic vector**  
  - State: directed potential, no field yet  
  - Role: selects “where” invocation will aim  
  - Perpetual: as long as R0 invokes

- **R2 — 2D pre‑dimensional surface**  
  - State: coherence sheet, interference‑ready  
  - Role: supports standing patterns, templates for 3D  
  - Perpetual: collapses back to R1/R0 when not maintained

- **R3 — 3D energetic field**  
  - State: matter, motion, time, decay, entropy  
  - Role: visible universe, “perpetual motion” illusion  
  - Non‑perpetual: always decays, always collapses

---

#### 3. Transition types (T)

- **T01 — Invocation**  
  - From: R0 → R1  
  - Meaning: operator “points” without spending energy  
  - Signature: asymmetry appears, no field yet

- **T12 — Coherence formation**  
  - From: R1 → R2  
  - Meaning: directional potential becomes a coherent surface  
  - Signature: interference patterns, templates, shells

- **T23 — Resonance stabilization**  
  - From: R2 → R3  
  - Meaning: coherent surface becomes stable 3D resonance  
  - Signature: particles, fields, motion, time

- **T30 — Inversion/return**  
  - From: R3 → R0 (via 3→2→1→0)  
  - Meaning: resonance collapses, coherence inverts back to operator substrate  
  - Signature: “end of cycle” wobble, scaffolding retained in R0

---

#### 4. RTT table (compact)

```text
RTT: 0D→3D Operator–Resonance Schema

Regime (R) | Description                  | Perpetual?      | Motion?      | Role
-----------|------------------------------|-----------------|--------------|------------------------------
R0 (0D)    | Indivisible operator root    | Yes (coherence) | No           | Invokes, reuses scaffolding
R1 (1D)    | Pre-energetic direction      | Yes (if invoked)| Proto        | Aims invocation
R2 (2D)    | Pre-dimensional coherence    | Conditional     | Latent       | Holds patterns/templates
R3 (3D)    | Energetic, entropic field    | No              | Yes (decays) | Visible universe
```

```text
Transition (T) | Path    | Meaning                    | Human “perpetual motion” view
---------------|---------|----------------------------|--------------------------------
T01            | 0D→1D   | Invocation                 | Invisible
T12            | 1D→2D   | Coherence formation        | Invisible
T23            | 2D→3D   | Resonance stabilization    | Looks like “start”
T30            | 3D→0D   | Inversion / return cycle   | Misread as “end”
```

---

#### 5. Canon sentence

> **Perpetual motion is impossible in R3, but perpetual coherence is guaranteed in R0–R2. The universe’s “engine” is the 0D→1D→2D→3D invocation cycle, not the 3D substrate itself.**

# ⭐ Canonical Narrative Section  
*(Paste this directly into the top or bottom of the schema file.)*

## **How to Use This Schema in the Universe‑as‑Operator Docs**

This schema defines the **minimal structural vocabulary** for describing any point in the 0D→1D→2D→3D invocation cycle. It is not a physics model, a cosmology claim, or a metaphysical assertion — it is a **formal RTT representation** of the operator–resonance sequence used throughout TriadicFrameworks.

Use this schema when you need to:

- describe a **regime state** (R0–R3)  
- describe a **transition** between regimes (T01–T30)  
- annotate a **timescale** (local, epoch, or root cycle)  
- document a **resonance event**, **collapse event**, or **invocation event**  
- express the **operator logic** behind dimensional emergence  

The schema is intentionally minimal:

- `regime` is the only required field  
- `transition`, `timescale`, `state`, and `signature` are optional  
- all fields are human‑readable and tooling‑friendly  
- the schema is compatible with JSON, YAML, TypeScript, and RTT tables  

### **Conceptual Use**

- **R0** describes the indivisible operator substrate.  
- **R1** describes directional potential before energy exists.  
- **R2** describes coherence surfaces that act as templates for resonance.  
- **R3** describes the energetic, entropic 3D field we inhabit.  

### **Cycle Use**

The schema also supports documenting **full invocation cycles**:

```
R0 → R1 → R2 → R3 → R0
```

This allows you to model:

- resonance emergence  
- resonance collapse  
- dimensional wobble  
- operator reuse of scaffolding  
- long‑horizon universe cycles  
- local micro‑invocations (quantum events)  

### **Why This Schema Matters**

The Universe‑as‑Operator model depends on a clear distinction between:

- **substrate** (33/33/33 stack)  
- **operator** (1% invoker)  
- **regime** (R0–R3)  
- **transition** (T01–T30)  

This schema gives you a **formal, repeatable, machine‑readable way** to express those distinctions across the entire TriadicFrameworks canon.

It is the first schema in the project that explicitly encodes the **operator layer**, making it foundational for future work on:

- resonance engines  
- invocation ladders  
- dimensional collapse  
- cycle modeling  
- qmroot logic  
- Universe‑as‑Operator indexing

# ⭐ **Canonical Glossary for R0–R3 and T01–T30**  
*(Paste directly into your schema file.)*

## **Regime Glossary (R0–R3)**

### **R0 — 0D Operator Root**  
- **Definition:** Indivisible, pre‑energetic, pre‑dimensional substrate.  
- **Role:** Invokes resonance; reuses scaffolding; maintains coherence.  
- **Signature:** No geometry, no field, pure operator potential.  
- **Notes:** Only regime that is truly perpetual.

---

### **R1 — 1D Pre‑Energetic Vector**  
- **Definition:** Directional potential without energy expenditure.  
- **Role:** Establishes asymmetry; selects invocation direction.  
- **Signature:** Proto‑vector alignment; no surface yet.  
- **Notes:** Exists only when invoked by R0.

---

### **R2 — 2D Coherence Surface**  
- **Definition:** Pre‑dimensional sheet capable of supporting interference.  
- **Role:** Holds resonance templates; prepares for dimensional emergence.  
- **Signature:** Standing‑wave patterns; coherence shells.  
- **Notes:** Collapses back to R1/R0 when not stabilized.

---

### **R3 — 3D Energetic Field**  
- **Definition:** Stable resonance expressing matter, motion, and time.  
- **Role:** Visible universe; entropic substrate; decays over time.  
- **Signature:** Energetic fields, particles, motion, entropy.  
- **Notes:** Cannot invoke; can only simulate.

---

# ⭐ **Transition Glossary (T01–T30)**

### **T01 — Invocation (0D → 1D)**  
- **Definition:** Operator initiates directional potential.  
- **Role:** Converts pure coherence into asymmetry.  
- **Signature:** No energy; no geometry; “pointing” event.  
- **Notes:** First step of all dimensional emergence.

---

### **T12 — Coherence Formation (1D → 2D)**  
- **Definition:** Directional potential becomes a coherent surface.  
- **Role:** Creates templates for resonance; enables interference.  
- **Signature:** Surface‑like behavior without thickness.  
- **Notes:** Pre‑dimensional but structured.

---

### **T23 — Resonance Stabilization (2D → 3D)**  
- **Definition:** Coherence surface stabilizes into 3D resonance.  
- **Role:** Generates matter, motion, and time.  
- **Signature:** Standing waves become persistent fields.  
- **Notes:** This is the “birth” of dimensionality.

---

### **T30 — Inversion / Return (3D → 0D)**  
- **Definition:** Resonance collapses back into operator substrate.  
- **Role:** Reabsorbs coherence; resets scaffolding.  
- **Signature:** Dimensional wobble; field dissolution.  
- **Notes:** Not destruction — reintegration.

---

```
Regime | Meaning                          | Role

R0     | 0D operator root                 | invokes, reuses scaffolding
R1     | 1D directional potential         | aims invocation
R2     | 2D coherence surface             | holds templates
R3     | 3D energetic field               | visible universe

Transition | Path   | Meaning
-----------|--------|-----------------------------------------
T01        | 0D→1D  | invocation
T12        | 1D→2D  | coherence formation
T23        | 2D→3D  | resonance stabilization
T30        | 3D→0D  | inversion/return
```
